########## FILE: .github/workflows/build.yml ##########
name: Build Cheetah APK

on:
  push:
    branches: [main, master]
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  build:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: "17"

      - uses: subosito/flutter-action@v2
        with:
          channel: stable
          cache: true

      # The repo only holds lib/, assets/ and pubspec.yaml.
      # The Android project (package com.cheetahapp) is generated here.
      - name: Generate Android project
        run: |
          cp -r lib /tmp/lib_bak
          flutter create . --org com --project-name cheetahapp --platforms android
          rm -rf lib && cp -r /tmp/lib_bak lib

      - name: Patch Android config
        run: |
          sed -i 's/minSdk = flutter.minSdkVersion/minSdk = 23/; s/minSdkVersion flutter.minSdkVersion/minSdkVersion 23/' android/app/build.gradle* || true
          M=android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml
          sed -i 's#<application#<uses-permission android:name="android.permission.INTERNET"/>\n    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS"/>\n    <application android:usesCleartextTraffic="true"#' $M
          sed -i 's/android:label="[^"]*"/android:label="Cheetah"/' $M

      - name: Restore app icon from text
        run: base64 -d assets/icon.b64 > assets/icon.png

      - run: flutter pub get

      - name: App icon
        run: dart run flutter_launcher_icons

      - name: Build release APK
        run: flutter build apk --release

      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: Cheetah-APK
          path: build/app/outputs/flutter-apk/app-release.apk

########## FILE: lib/main.dart ##########
import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
import 'package:firebase_core/firebase_core.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import 'auth.dart';
import 'firebase_options.dart';
import 'shell.dart';
import 'theme.dart';

Future<void> main() async {
  WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();
  await Firebase.initializeApp(options: DefaultFirebaseOptions.android);
  runApp(const CheetahApp());
}

class CheetahApp extends StatelessWidget {
  const CheetahApp({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return MaterialApp(
      title: 'Cheetah',
      debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
      theme: ThemeData(
        useMaterial3: true,
        splashFactory: InkSparkle.splashFactory,
        progressIndicatorTheme: const ProgressIndicatorThemeData(color: kRed),
        snackBarTheme: const SnackBarThemeData(behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating),
        brightness: Brightness.dark,
        scaffoldBackgroundColor: kBg,
        colorScheme: const ColorScheme.dark(primary: kRed, surface: kCard),
        appBarTheme: const AppBarTheme(backgroundColor: kBg, elevation: 0),
        navigationBarTheme: NavigationBarThemeData(
          backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF111115),
          indicatorColor: kRed.withOpacity(0.25),
        ),
      ),
      home: StreamBuilder<User?>(
        stream: FirebaseAuth.instance.authStateChanges(),
        builder: (context, snap) {
          if (snap.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) return const Splash();
          final u = snap.data;
          if (u == null) return const AuthScreen();
          return Boot(key: ValueKey(u.uid));
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}

########## FILE: lib/store.dart ##########
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;

typedef Item = Map<String, dynamic>;

final db = FirebaseFirestore.instance;

/// TMDB / OMDB access. Keys are stored in Firestore (config/api) and set
/// from the admin panel, so they never live in the source code.
class Api {
  static String tmdbKey = '';
  static String omdbKey = '';

  static Future<void> load() async {
    try {
      final d = await db.collection('config').doc('api').get();
      final m = d.data() ?? {};
      tmdbKey = '${m['tmdbKey'] ?? ''}'.trim();
      omdbKey = '${m['omdbKey'] ?? ''}'.trim();
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  static Future<Map<String, dynamic>?> tmdb(String path,
      [Map<String, String> q = const {}]) async {
    if (tmdbKey.isEmpty) return null;
    try {
      final uri =
          Uri.https('api.themoviedb.org', '/3/$path', {'api_key': tmdbKey, ...q});
      final r = await http.get(uri).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 15));
      if (r.statusCode == 200) {
        return Map<String, dynamic>.from(jsonDecode(r.body) as Map);
      }
    } catch (_) {}
    return null;
  }

  static Future<Map<String, dynamic>?> omdb(String imdbId) async {
    if (omdbKey.isEmpty || imdbId.isEmpty) return null;
    try {
      final uri = Uri.https('www.omdbapi.com', '/', {'apikey': omdbKey, 'i': imdbId});
      final r = await http.get(uri).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 15));
      if (r.statusCode == 200) {
        final j = Map<String, dynamic>.from(jsonDecode(r.body) as Map);
        if (j['Response'] == 'True') return j;
      }
    } catch (_) {}
    return null;
  }
}

class _Col {
  final String name;
  final ValueNotifier<List<Item>> notifier;
  final int Function(Item, Item) cmp;
  _Col(this.name, this.notifier, this.cmp);
}

/// Live copies of the Firestore collections the app needs.
class Store {
  static final content = ValueNotifier<List<Item>>([]);
  static final slides = ValueNotifier<List<Item>>([]);
  static final ads = ValueNotifier<List<Item>>([]);
  static final servers = ValueNotifier<List<Item>>([]);
  static final popups = ValueNotifier<List<Item>>([]);
  static final notifs = ValueNotifier<List<Item>>([]);

  /// false until the first batch of content arrives (drives the skeletons).
  static final ready = ValueNotifier<bool>(false);

  static final List<StreamSubscription> _subs = [];

  static int _ts(Item m) {
    final t = m['createdAt'];
    return t is Timestamp ? t.millisecondsSinceEpoch : 0;
  }

  static num _n(dynamic v) => v is num ? v : 0;

  static int _newest(Item a, Item b) => _ts(b).compareTo(_ts(a));

  static int _byOrder(Item a, Item b) => _n(a['order']).compareTo(_n(b['order']));

  static final List<_Col> _cols = [
    _Col('content', content, _newest),
    _Col('slides', slides, _byOrder),
    _Col('ads', ads, _newest),
    _Col('servers', servers, _byOrder),
    _Col('popups', popups, _newest),
    _Col('notifications', notifs, _newest),
  ];

  static List<Item> _read(QuerySnapshot<Map<String, dynamic>> s, int Function(Item, Item) cmp) {
    final l = s.docs.map((d) => <String, dynamic>{...d.data(), 'id': d.id}).toList();
    l.sort(cmp);
    return l;
  }

  static void start() {
    if (_subs.isNotEmpty) return;
    for (final c in _cols) {
      _subs.add(db.collection(c.name).snapshots().listen((s) {
        c.notifier.value = _read(s, c.cmp);
        if (c.name == 'content') ready.value = true;
      }, onError: (_) {
        if (c.name == 'content') ready.value = true;
      }));
    }
  }

  static void stop() {
    for (final s in _subs) {
      s.cancel();
    }
    _subs.clear();
    ready.value = false;
  }

  static Future<bool> _fetch(_Col c) async {
    try {
      final s = await db
          .collection(c.name)
          .get(const GetOptions(source: Source.server))
          .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 15));
      c.notifier.value = _read(s, c.cmp);
      if (c.name == 'content') ready.value = true;
      return true;
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  /// Pull-to-refresh: re-reads every collection straight from the server and
  /// reloads the API keys. Returns false if the server could not be reached.
  static Future<bool> refresh() async {
    final r = await Future.wait<bool>([
      for (final c in _cols) _fetch(c),
      Api.load().then((_) => true),
    ]);
    return r.every((e) => e);
  }
}

########## FILE: lib/widgets.dart ##########
import 'package:cached_network_image/cached_network_image.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:url_launcher/url_launcher.dart';

import 'detail.dart';
import 'store.dart';
import 'theme.dart';

Future<void> openLink(String url) async {
  if (url.trim().isEmpty) return;
  try {
    final u = Uri.tryParse(url.trim());
    if (u != null) await launchUrl(u, mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication);
  } catch (_) {}
}

void openItem(BuildContext c, Item it) => Navigator.push(
      c,
      PageRouteBuilder(
        transitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 280),
        reverseTransitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 220),
        pageBuilder: (_, __, ___) => DetailScreen(item: it),
        transitionsBuilder: (_, a, __, child) => FadeTransition(
          opacity: CurvedAnimation(parent: a, curve: Curves.easeOut),
          child: SlideTransition(
            position: Tween(begin: const Offset(0, 0.04), end: Offset.zero)
                .animate(CurvedAnimation(parent: a, curve: Curves.easeOutCubic)),
            child: child,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );

// ───────────────────────── Pull to refresh ─────────────────────────

/// Wrap any scrollable (with AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics) to get
/// pull-to-refresh that re-reads everything from the server.
class Pull extends StatelessWidget {
  final Widget child;
  const Pull({super.key, required this.child});

  Future<void> _go(BuildContext c) async {
    HapticFeedback.lightImpact();
    final ok = await Store.refresh();
    if (!c.mounted) return;
    final m = ScaffoldMessenger.of(c);
    m.hideCurrentSnackBar();
    m.showSnackBar(SnackBar(
      behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
      duration: const Duration(seconds: 2),
      backgroundColor: kCard2,
      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
      content: Row(children: [
        Icon(ok ? Icons.check_circle_rounded : Icons.wifi_off_rounded,
            size: 18, color: ok ? Colors.greenAccent : Colors.orangeAccent),
        const SizedBox(width: 10),
        Text(ok ? 'Up to date' : 'Couldn\'t reach the server'),
      ]),
    ));
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return RefreshIndicator(
      color: kRed,
      backgroundColor: kCard2,
      displacement: 48,
      onRefresh: () => _go(context),
      child: child,
    );
  }
}

// ───────────────────────── Images ─────────────────────────

class Img extends StatelessWidget {
  final String? url;
  final BoxFit fit;
  const Img(this.url, {super.key, this.fit = BoxFit.cover});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (url == null || url!.isEmpty) {
      return Container(color: kCard, child: const Icon(Icons.movie_outlined, color: Colors.white24));
    }
    return CachedNetworkImage(
      imageUrl: url!,
      fit: fit,
      fadeInDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 250),
      placeholder: (_, __) => Container(color: kCard),
      errorWidget: (_, __, ___) => Container(
        color: kCard,
        child: const Icon(Icons.broken_image_outlined, color: Colors.white24),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ───────────────────────── Skeleton loading ─────────────────────────

class Skeleton extends StatefulWidget {
  final double? width;
  final double height;
  final double radius;
  const Skeleton({super.key, this.width, required this.height, this.radius = 12});

  @override
  State<Skeleton> createState() => _SkeletonState();
}

class _SkeletonState extends State<Skeleton> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late final AnimationController _c =
      AnimationController(vsync: this, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 1300))..repeat();

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _c.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AnimatedBuilder(
      animation: _c,
      builder: (_, __) {
        final t = _c.value;
        return Container(
          width: widget.width,
          height: widget.height,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(widget.radius),
            gradient: LinearGradient(
              begin: Alignment(-1.5 + 3 * t, 0),
              end: Alignment(-0.5 + 3 * t, 0),
              colors: const [kCard, kCard2, kCard],
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

class HomeSkeleton extends StatelessWidget {
  const HomeSkeleton({super.key});

  Widget _row() => Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 22, 16, 12),
            child: Skeleton(width: 120, height: 18, radius: 6),
          ),
          SizedBox(
            height: 190,
            child: ListView.separated(
              physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
              scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
              itemCount: 4,
              separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(width: 12),
              itemBuilder: (_, __) => const Skeleton(width: 110, height: 165),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      );

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ListView(
      physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
      children: [
        const Padding(
          padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 10, 16, 14),
          child: Row(children: [
            Skeleton(width: 40, height: 40, radius: 12),
            SizedBox(width: 12),
            Skeleton(width: 130, height: 18, radius: 6),
          ]),
        ),
        const Padding(
          padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
          child: Skeleton(height: 210, radius: 22),
        ),
        _row(),
        _row(),
      ],
    );
  }
}

// ───────────────────────── Cards & grids ─────────────────────────

class PosterCard extends StatefulWidget {
  final Item item;
  final double? width;
  const PosterCard({super.key, required this.item, this.width});

  @override
  State<PosterCard> createState() => _PosterCardState();
}

class _PosterCardState extends State<PosterCard> {
  bool _down = false;

  void _set(bool v) {
    if (mounted && _down != v) setState(() => _down = v);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final item = widget.item;
    final r = item['rating'] is num ? (item['rating'] as num).toDouble() : 0.0;
    final year = '${item['year'] ?? ''}';
    final isTv = item['type'] == 'tv';
    return GestureDetector(
      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
      onTapDown: (_) => _set(true),
      onTapUp: (_) => _set(false),
      onTapCancel: () => _set(false),
      onTap: () => openItem(context, item),
      child: AnimatedScale(
        scale: _down ? 0.95 : 1,
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 120),
        curve: Curves.easeOut,
        child: SizedBox(
          width: widget.width,
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              AspectRatio(
                aspectRatio: 2 / 3,
                child: Container(
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                    border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
                    boxShadow: const [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black54, blurRadius: 10, offset: Offset(0, 4))],
                  ),
                  child: ClipRRect(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13),
                    child: Stack(
                      fit: StackFit.expand,
                      children: [
                        Img(item['poster']?.toString()),
                        const DecoratedBox(
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            gradient: LinearGradient(
                              begin: Alignment.topCenter,
                              end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
                              colors: [Colors.transparent, Color(0x99000000)],
                              stops: [0.65, 1],
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                        if (r > 0)
                          Positioned(
                            top: 6,
                            right: 6,
                            child: Container(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 3),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.65),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                              ),
                              child: Row(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
                                const Icon(Icons.star_rounded, size: 12, color: Colors.amber),
                                const SizedBox(width: 2),
                                Text(r.toStringAsFixed(1),
                                    style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
                              ]),
                            ),
                          ),
                        if (isTv)
                          Positioned(
                            left: 6,
                            bottom: 6,
                            child: Container(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                gradient: kRedGradient,
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                              ),
                              child: const Text('SERIES',
                                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 9, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, letterSpacing: 0.6)),
                            ),
                          ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Text(
                '${item['title'] ?? ''}',
                maxLines: 1,
                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
              ),
              if (year.isNotEmpty)
                Text(year, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: Colors.white38)),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

const kGridDelegate = SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(
  crossAxisCount: 3,
  mainAxisSpacing: 14,
  crossAxisSpacing: 10,
  childAspectRatio: 0.5,
);

class _FadeIn extends StatelessWidget {
  final int index;
  final Widget child;
  const _FadeIn({required this.index, required this.child});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return TweenAnimationBuilder<double>(
      tween: Tween(begin: 0, end: 1),
      duration: Duration(milliseconds: 280 + (index % 9) * 45),
      curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
      builder: (_, v, c) => Opacity(
        opacity: v,
        child: Transform.translate(offset: Offset(0, 14 * (1 - v)), child: c),
      ),
      child: child,
    );
  }
}

SliverGrid posterSliver(List<Item> items) {
  return SliverGrid(
    gridDelegate: kGridDelegate,
    delegate: SliverChildBuilderDelegate(
      (_, i) => _FadeIn(index: i, child: PosterCard(item: items[i])),
      childCount: items.length,
    ),
  );
}

Widget posterGrid(List<Item> items) {
  return GridView.builder(
    physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
    keyboardDismissBehavior: ScrollViewKeyboardDismissBehavior.onDrag,
    padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 8, 16, 24),
    gridDelegate: kGridDelegate,
    itemCount: items.length,
    itemBuilder: (_, i) => _FadeIn(index: i, child: PosterCard(item: items[i])),
  );
}

class EmptyState extends StatelessWidget {
  final IconData icon;
  final String title;
  final String? subtitle;
  const EmptyState({super.key, required this.icon, required this.title, this.subtitle});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Center(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(32),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Container(
              width: 76,
              height: 76,
              decoration: const BoxDecoration(color: kCard, shape: BoxShape.circle),
              child: Icon(icon, size: 34, color: Colors.white38),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Text(title, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
            if (subtitle != null) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              Text(subtitle!, textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54)),
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// Keeps the screen pull-to-refresh-able even when there is nothing to show.
Widget emptyScrollable(Widget child) => Pull(
      child: LayoutBuilder(
        builder: (_, box) => ListView(
          physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
          children: [SizedBox(height: box.maxHeight, child: child)],
        ),
      ),
    );

// ───────────────────────── Buttons & headers ─────────────────────────

class GradientButton extends StatelessWidget {
  final IconData icon;
  final String label;
  final VoidCallback onTap;
  final double height;
  const GradientButton({
    super.key,
    required this.icon,
    required this.label,
    required this.onTap,
    this.height = 50,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Material(
      color: Colors.transparent,
      child: Ink(
        height: height,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          gradient: kRedGradient,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: kRed.withOpacity(0.35), blurRadius: 16, offset: const Offset(0, 6))],
        ),
        child: InkWell(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          onTap: onTap,
          child: Center(
            child: Row(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                Icon(icon, size: 24),
                const SizedBox(width: 6),
                Text(label, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

Widget sectionHeader(String title, {VoidCallback? onMore}) => Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 22, 8, 10),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            width: 4,
            height: 18,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(gradient: kRedGradient, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2)),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Expanded(
            child: Text(title, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
          ),
          if (onMore != null)
            TextButton(
              onPressed: onMore,
              style: TextButton.styleFrom(foregroundColor: Colors.white60, visualDensity: VisualDensity.compact),
              child: const Row(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
                Text('See all', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12.5)),
                Icon(Icons.chevron_right_rounded, size: 18),
              ]),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );

Widget hRow(BuildContext c, String title, List<Item> items) {
  if (items.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();
  return Column(
    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
    children: [
      sectionHeader(title,
          onMore: items.length > 3
              ? () => Navigator.push(c, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => SeeAllScreen(title: title, items: items)))
              : null),
      SizedBox(
        height: 232,
        child: ListView.separated(
          scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
          physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
          itemCount: items.length,
          separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(width: 12),
          itemBuilder: (_, i) => PosterCard(item: items[i], width: 110),
        ),
      ),
    ],
  );
}

class SeeAllScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final List<Item> items;
  const SeeAllScreen({super.key, required this.title, required this.items});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text(title, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800))),
      body: Pull(child: posterGrid(items)),
    );
  }
}

/// Banner ads managed from the admin panel.
class AdBanner extends StatelessWidget {
  final String placement;
  const AdBanner(this.placement, {super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ValueListenableBuilder<List<Item>>(
      valueListenable: Store.ads,
      builder: (_, ads, __) {
        final l = ads
            .where((a) => a['active'] != false && '${a['placement'] ?? 'home'}' == placement)
            .toList();
        if (l.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();
        return Column(
          children: [
            for (final a in l)
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 8, 16, 8),
                child: GestureDetector(
                  onTap: () => openLink('${a['link'] ?? ''}'),
                  child: ClipRRect(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                    child: Stack(
                      children: [
                        AspectRatio(aspectRatio: 16 / 5, child: Img(a['image']?.toString())),
                        Positioned(
                          top: 6,
                          left: 6,
                          child: Container(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 1),
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: Colors.black54,
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                            ),
                            child: const Text('Ad', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10)),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

########## FILE: lib/auth.dart ##########
import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import 'store.dart';
import 'theme.dart';

class AuthScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const AuthScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<AuthScreen> createState() => _AuthScreenState();
}

class _AuthScreenState extends State<AuthScreen> {
  bool login = true;
  bool busy = false;
  bool hide = true;
  String? err;
  final name = TextEditingController();
  final email = TextEditingController();
  final pass = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void dispose() {
    name.dispose();
    email.dispose();
    pass.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  String _msg(FirebaseAuthException e) {
    switch (e.code) {
      case 'invalid-email':
        return 'Please enter a valid email address.';
      case 'user-not-found':
      case 'wrong-password':
      case 'invalid-credential':
        return 'Incorrect email or password.';
      case 'email-already-in-use':
        return 'An account with this email already exists.';
      case 'weak-password':
        return 'Password must be at least 6 characters.';
      case 'network-request-failed':
        return 'No internet connection.';
      case 'too-many-requests':
        return 'Too many attempts. Try again later.';
      default:
        return e.message ?? 'Something went wrong.';
    }
  }

  Future<void> submit() async {
    FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
    setState(() {
      busy = true;
      err = null;
    });
    try {
      final auth = FirebaseAuth.instance;
      if (login) {
        await auth.signInWithEmailAndPassword(email: email.text.trim(), password: pass.text);
      } else {
        if (name.text.trim().isEmpty) {
          throw FirebaseAuthException(code: 'custom', message: 'Please enter your name.');
        }
        final cr = await auth.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(
            email: email.text.trim(), password: pass.text);
        await cr.user?.updateDisplayName(name.text.trim());
        try {
          await db.collection('users').doc(cr.user!.uid).set({
            'name': name.text.trim(),
            'email': email.text.trim(),
            'createdAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
          });
        } catch (_) {}
      }
    } on FirebaseAuthException catch (e) {
      err = _msg(e);
    } catch (e) {
      err = 'Something went wrong. Try again.';
    }
    if (mounted) setState(() => busy = false);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      body: SafeArea(
        child: Center(
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
            child: ConstrainedBox(
              constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 420),
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  ClipRRect(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
                    child: Image.asset('assets/icon.png', width: 96, height: 96),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 14),
                  const Text('Cheetah',
                      style: TextStyle(fontSize: 30, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, letterSpacing: 0.5)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text(login ? 'Welcome back' : 'Create your account',
                      style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 28),
                  if (!login) ...[
                    _field(name, 'Full name', Icons.person_outline),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  ],
                  _field(email, 'Email', Icons.mail_outline, type: TextInputType.emailAddress),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  TextField(
                    controller: pass,
                    obscureText: hide,
                    decoration: _dec('Password', Icons.lock_outline).copyWith(
                      suffixIcon: IconButton(
                        icon: Icon(hide ? Icons.visibility_off_outlined : Icons.visibility_outlined),
                        onPressed: () => setState(() => hide = !hide),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  if (err != null) ...[
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    Text(err!, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.redAccent), textAlign: TextAlign.center),
                  ],
                  const SizedBox(height: 20),
                  SizedBox(
                    width: double.infinity,
                    height: 50,
                    child: FilledButton(
                      style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
                        backgroundColor: kRed,
                        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                      ),
                      onPressed: busy ? null : submit,
                      child: busy
                          ? const SizedBox(
                              width: 22, height: 22, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2.5))
                          : Text(login ? 'Log in' : 'Sign up',
                              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 14),
                  TextButton(
                    onPressed: () => setState(() {
                      login = !login;
                      err = null;
                    }),
                    child: Text(login ? "Don't have an account? Sign up" : 'Already have an account? Log in'),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _field(TextEditingController c, String label, IconData icon, {TextInputType? type}) =>
      TextField(controller: c, keyboardType: type, decoration: _dec(label, icon));

  InputDecoration _dec(String label, IconData icon) => InputDecoration(
        labelText: label,
        prefixIcon: Icon(icon),
        filled: true,
        fillColor: kCard,
        border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14), borderSide: BorderSide.none),
      );
}

import 'package:firebase_messaging/firebase_messaging.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

import 'browse.dart';

class Splash extends StatelessWidget {
  const Splash({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      body: Center(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            ClipRRect(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
              child: Image.asset('assets/icon.png', width: 96, height: 96),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            const SizedBox(width: 24, height: 24, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2.5)),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// Runs after login: starts live data listeners, loads API keys, sets up push.
class Boot extends StatefulWidget {
  const Boot({super.key});

  @override
  State<Boot> createState() => _BootState();
}

class _BootState extends State<Boot> {
  late final Future<void> _f = _init();

  Future<void> _init() async {
    Store.start();
    UserData.start();
    await Api.load();
    try {
      final m = FirebaseMessaging.instance;
      await m.requestPermission();
      await m.subscribeToTopic('all');
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    Store.stop();
    UserData.stop();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return FutureBuilder<void>(
      future: _f,
      builder: (_, s) => s.connectionState == ConnectionState.done ? const Shell() : const Splash(),
    );
  }
}

class Shell extends StatefulWidget {
  const Shell({super.key});

  @override
  State<Shell> createState() => _ShellState();
}

class _ShellState extends State<Shell> {
  int tab = 0;
  bool _popupDone = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    Store.popups.addListener(_checkPopup);
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => _checkPopup());
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    Store.popups.removeListener(_checkPopup);
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _checkPopup() async {
    if (_popupDone || !mounted) return;
    final list = Store.popups.value.where((p) => p['active'] == true).toList();
    if (list.isEmpty) return;
    _popupDone = true;
    final p = list.first;
    final always = p['showAlways'] == true;
    final sp = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    final seen = sp.getStringList('seen_popups') ?? <String>[];
    if (!always && seen.contains('${p['id']}')) return;
    if (!mounted) return;
    await showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => Dialog(
        backgroundColor: kCard,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
        clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              if ('${p['image'] ?? ''}'.isNotEmpty)
                AspectRatio(aspectRatio: 16 / 9, child: Img(p['image']?.toString())),
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(18),
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    if ('${p['title'] ?? ''}'.isNotEmpty)
                      Text('${p['title']}',
                          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                          style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
                    if ('${p['message'] ?? ''}'.isNotEmpty) ...[
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      Text('${p['message']}',
                          textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70)),
                    ],
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    Row(
                      children: [
                        Expanded(
                          child: OutlinedButton(
                            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
                            child: const Text('Close'),
                          ),
                        ),
                        if ('${p['link'] ?? ''}'.isNotEmpty) ...[
                          const SizedBox(width: 10),
                          Expanded(
                            child: FilledButton(
                              style: FilledButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: kRed),
                              onPressed: () {
                                Navigator.pop(ctx);
                                openLink('${p['link']}');
                              },
                              child: Text('${p['buttonText'] ?? ''}'.isEmpty ? 'Open' : '${p['buttonText']}'),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ],
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
    if (!always) {
      seen.add('${p['id']}');
      await sp.setStringList('seen_popups', seen);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final pages = [
      HomeScreen(onSearch: () => setState(() => tab = 2)),
      const CategoryScreen(),
      const SearchScreen(),
      const ProfileScreen(),
    ];
    return Scaffold(
      body: IndexedStack(index: tab, children: pages),
      bottomNavigationBar: _NavBar(index: tab, onTap: (i) => setState(() => tab = i)),
    );
  }
}

class _NavBar extends StatelessWidget {
  final int index;
  final ValueChanged<int> onTap;
  const _NavBar({required this.index, required this.onTap});

  static const _items = [
    (Icons.home_rounded, Icons.home_outlined, 'Home'),
    (Icons.grid_view_rounded, Icons.grid_view_outlined, 'Explore'),
    (Icons.search_rounded, Icons.search_rounded, 'Search'),
    (Icons.person_rounded, Icons.person_outline_rounded, 'Profile'),
  ];

  Widget _item(int i) {
    final sel = i == index;
    final it = _items[i];
    return GestureDetector(
      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
      onTap: () {
        if (!sel) HapticFeedback.selectionClick();
        onTap(i);
      },
      child: AnimatedContainer(
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 280),
        curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
        padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: sel ? 16 : 12, vertical: 10),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          gradient: sel ? kRedGradient : null,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22),
          boxShadow: sel ? [BoxShadow(color: kRed.withOpacity(0.4), blurRadius: 14, offset: const Offset(0, 4))] : null,
        ),
        child: Row(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Icon(sel ? it.$1 : it.$2, size: 24, color: sel ? Colors.white : Colors.white54),
            AnimatedSize(
              duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 280),
              curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
              child: sel
                  ? Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8),
                      child: Text(it.$3, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 13)),
                    )
                  : const SizedBox.shrink(),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SafeArea(
      top: false,
      child: Container(
        margin: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 4, 16, 10),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 8),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: kCard.withOpacity(0.96),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(30),
          border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
          boxShadow: const [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black87, blurRadius: 20, offset: Offset(0, 6))],
        ),
        child: Row(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround,
          children: [for (var i = 0; i < _items.length; i++) _item(i)],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

########## FILE: lib/home.dart ##########
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

import 'profile.dart';
import 'store.dart';
import 'theme.dart';
import 'widgets.dart';

class HomeScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  final VoidCallback? onSearch;
  const HomeScreen({super.key, this.onSearch});

  static double _rating(Item e) => e['rating'] is num ? (e['rating'] as num).toDouble() : 0.0;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SafeArea(
      bottom: false,
      child: ValueListenableBuilder<bool>(
        valueListenable: Store.ready,
        builder: (_, ready, __) {
          if (!ready) return const HomeSkeleton();
          return ValueListenableBuilder<List<Item>>(
            valueListenable: Store.content,
            builder: (_, all, __) {
              List<Item> of(String t) => all.where((e) => e['type'] == t).toList();
              final top = all.where((e) => _rating(e) > 0).toList()
                ..sort((a, b) => _rating(b).compareTo(_rating(a)));
              return Pull(
                child: ListView(
                  physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(parent: BouncingScrollPhysics()),
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 28),
                  children: [
                    _Header(onSearch: onSearch),
                    const HeroSlider(),
                    const AdBanner('home'),
                    if (all.isEmpty)
                      const SizedBox(
                        height: 280,
                        child: EmptyState(
                          icon: Icons.movie_filter_outlined,
                          title: 'No content yet',
                          subtitle: 'Pull down to refresh or check back soon!',
                        ),
                      ),
                    hRow(context, 'Latest', all.take(15).toList()),
                    hRow(context, 'Top Rated', top.take(15).toList()),
                    hRow(context, 'Movies', of('movie')),
                    hRow(context, 'Series', of('tv')),
                    hRow(context, 'More', of('other')),
                  ],
                ),
              );
            },
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _Header extends StatefulWidget {
  final VoidCallback? onSearch;
  const _Header({this.onSearch});

  @override
  State<_Header> createState() => _HeaderState();
}

class _HeaderState extends State<_Header> {
  int _seen = 0;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    SharedPreferences.getInstance().then((sp) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _seen = sp.getInt('seen_notifs') ?? 0);
    });
  }

  String get _greeting {
    final h = DateTime.now().hour;
    if (h < 5) return 'Late night';
    if (h < 12) return 'Good morning';
    if (h < 17) return 'Good afternoon';
    return 'Good evening';
  }

  Future<void> _openNotifs() async {
    final n = Store.notifs.value.length;
    final sp = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await sp.setInt('seen_notifs', n);
    if (mounted) setState(() => _seen = n);
    if (!mounted) return;
    Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const NotificationsScreen()));
  }

  Widget _circleBtn(IconData i, VoidCallback on, {bool dot = false}) => Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8),
        child: Material(
          color: kCard,
          shape: const CircleBorder(side: BorderSide(color: Colors.white10)),
          child: InkWell(
            customBorder: const CircleBorder(),
            onTap: on,
            child: SizedBox(
              width: 42,
              height: 42,
              child: Stack(
                alignment: Alignment.center,
                children: [
                  Icon(i, size: 22),
                  if (dot)
                    Positioned(
                      top: 10,
                      right: 11,
                      child: Container(
                        width: 9,
                        height: 9,
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: kRed,
                          shape: BoxShape.circle,
                          border: Border.all(color: kCard, width: 1.5),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      );

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final name = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser?.displayName ?? '';
    final first = name.trim().isEmpty ? 'Cheetah' : name.trim().split(' ').first;
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 8, 12, 14),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(2),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(gradient: kRedGradient, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13)),
            child: ClipRRect(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(11),
              child: Image.asset('assets/icon.png', width: 38, height: 38),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(_greeting, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: Colors.white54)),
                Text(first,
                    maxLines: 1,
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, height: 1.15)),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          _circleBtn(Icons.search_rounded, () => widget.onSearch?.call()),
          ValueListenableBuilder<List<Item>>(
            valueListenable: Store.notifs,
            builder: (_, l, __) =>
                _circleBtn(Icons.notifications_none_rounded, _openNotifs, dot: l.length > _seen),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _Slide {
  final String image, title, contentId, link, meta;
  _Slide(this.image, this.title, this.contentId, this.link, this.meta);
}

class HeroSlider extends StatefulWidget {
  const HeroSlider({super.key});

  @override
  State<HeroSlider> createState() => _HeroSliderState();
}

class _HeroSliderState extends State<HeroSlider> {
  static const _vf = 0.88;
  final _pc = PageController(viewportFraction: _vf);
  Timer? _t;
  int _i = 0;
  DateTime _touched = DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(0);

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _t = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 5), (_) {
      if (!_pc.hasClients) return;
      if (DateTime.now().difference(_touched) < const Duration(seconds: 7)) return;
      final n = _items().length;
      if (n < 2) return;
      _pc.animateToPage((_i + 1) % n,
          duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 550), curve: Curves.easeInOutCubic);
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _t?.cancel();
    _pc.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Item? _content(String id) =>
      id.isEmpty ? null : Store.content.value.where((e) => e['id'] == id).firstOrNull;

  String _meta(Item? it) {
    if (it == null) return '';
    final p = <String>[];
    final y = '${it['year'] ?? ''}';
    if (y.isNotEmpty) p.add(y);
    p.add(it['type'] == 'tv' ? 'Series' : (it['type'] == 'movie' ? 'Movie' : 'Featured'));
    final r = it['rating'] is num ? (it['rating'] as num).toDouble() : 0.0;
    if (r > 0) p.add('★ ${r.toStringAsFixed(1)}');
    return p.join('  ·  ');
  }

  List<_Slide> _items() {
    final s = Store.slides.value
        .where((e) => e['active'] != false && '${e['image'] ?? ''}'.isNotEmpty)
        .toList();
    if (s.isNotEmpty) {
      return s.map((e) {
        final id = '${e['contentId'] ?? ''}';
        return _Slide('${e['image']}', '${e['title'] ?? ''}', id, '${e['link'] ?? ''}', _meta(_content(id)));
      }).toList();
    }
    return Store.content.value
        .where((e) => '${e['backdrop'] ?? ''}'.isNotEmpty)
        .take(5)
        .map((e) => _Slide('${e['backdrop']}', '${e['title'] ?? ''}', '${e['id']}', '', _meta(e)))
        .toList();
  }

  void _tap(_Slide s) {
    final it = _content(s.contentId);
    if (it != null) {
      openItem(context, it);
      return;
    }
    openLink(s.link);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AnimatedBuilder(
      animation: Listenable.merge([Store.slides, Store.content]),
      builder: (_, __) {
        final items = _items();
        if (items.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();
        return LayoutBuilder(
          builder: (_, box) {
            final h = box.maxWidth * _vf * 10 / 16;
            return Column(
              children: [
                SizedBox(
                  height: h,
                  child: Listener(
                    onPointerDown: (_) => _touched = DateTime.now(),
                    child: PageView.builder(
                      controller: _pc,
                      physics: const PageScrollPhysics(parent: BouncingScrollPhysics()),
                      itemCount: items.length,
                      onPageChanged: (i) => setState(() => _i = i),
                      itemBuilder: (_, i) => AnimatedBuilder(
                        animation: _pc,
                        builder: (_, child) {
                          var page = _i.toDouble();
                          if (_pc.hasClients && _pc.position.haveDimensions) page = _pc.page ?? page;
                          final d = (page - i).abs().clamp(0.0, 1.0);
                          return Transform.scale(
                            scale: 1 - d * 0.07,
                            child: Opacity(opacity: 1 - d * 0.35, child: child),
                          );
                        },
                        child: _slide(items[i]),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    for (var i = 0; i < items.length; i++)
                      AnimatedContainer(
                        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 280),
                        curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
                        margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 3),
                        width: i == _i ? 22 : 6,
                        height: 6,
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          gradient: i == _i ? kRedGradient : null,
                          color: i == _i ? null : Colors.white24,
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(3),
                        ),
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _slide(_Slide s) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 5),
      child: GestureDetector(
        onTap: () => _tap(s),
        child: Container(
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22),
            border: Border.all(color: Colors.white12),
            boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: kRed.withOpacity(0.18), blurRadius: 22, offset: const Offset(0, 8))],
          ),
          child: ClipRRect(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(21),
            child: Stack(
              fit: StackFit.expand,
              children: [
                Img(s.image),
                const DecoratedBox(
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    gradient: LinearGradient(
                      begin: Alignment.topCenter,
                      end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
                      colors: [Colors.transparent, Color(0xF2000000)],
                      stops: [0.35, 1],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                Positioned(
                  left: 16,
                  right: 16,
                  bottom: 14,
                  child: Row(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                        child: Column(
                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                          children: [
                            if (s.title.isNotEmpty)
                              Text(
                                s.title,
                                maxLines: 2,
                                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 19, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, height: 1.15),
                              ),
                            if (s.meta.isNotEmpty) ...[
                              const SizedBox(height: 4),
                              Text(s.meta, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: Colors.white70)),
                            ],
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 10),
                      Container(
                        width: 44,
                        height: 44,
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          gradient: kRedGradient,
                          shape: BoxShape.circle,
                          boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: kRed.withOpacity(0.5), blurRadius: 14)],
                        ),
                        child: const Icon(Icons.play_arrow_rounded, size: 28),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

########## FILE: lib/browse.dart ##########
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

import 'store.dart';
import 'theme.dart';
import 'widgets.dart';

class _Pill extends StatelessWidget {
  final String label;
  final bool selected;
  final VoidCallback onTap;
  const _Pill({required this.label, required this.selected, required this.onTap});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: onTap,
      child: AnimatedContainer(
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 220),
        curve: Curves.easeOut,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          gradient: selected ? kRedGradient : null,
          color: selected ? null : kCard,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22),
          border: Border.all(color: selected ? Colors.transparent : Colors.white10),
        ),
        child: Text(
          label,
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 13,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
            color: selected ? Colors.white : Colors.white70,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

Widget _pillRow(List<MapEntry<String, String>> items, String sel, void Function(String) on) {
  return SizedBox(
    height: 42,
    child: ListView.separated(
      scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
      physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 3),
      itemCount: items.length,
      separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(width: 8),
      itemBuilder: (_, i) => _Pill(
        label: items[i].value,
        selected: sel == items[i].key,
        onTap: () => on(items[i].key),
      ),
    ),
  );
}

class CategoryScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const CategoryScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<CategoryScreen> createState() => _CategoryScreenState();
}

class _CategoryScreenState extends State<CategoryScreen> {
  String type = 'all';
  String genre = '';

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SafeArea(
      bottom: false,
      child: ValueListenableBuilder<List<Item>>(
        valueListenable: Store.content,
        builder: (_, all, __) {
          final genres = <String>{};
          for (final e in all) {
            final g = e['genres'];
            if (g is List) genres.addAll(g.map((x) => '$x'));
          }
          final list = all.where((e) {
            final okType = type == 'all' || e['type'] == type;
            final g = e['genres'];
            final okGenre = genre.isEmpty || (g is List && g.contains(genre));
            return okType && okGenre;
          }).toList();
          const types = {'all': 'All', 'movie': 'Movies', 'tv': 'Series', 'other': 'More'};
          return Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 14, 16, 10),
                child: Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                  children: [
                    const Expanded(
                      child: Text('Explore', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 26, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
                    ),
                    AnimatedSwitcher(
                      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 200),
                      child: Text(
                        '${list.length} titles',
                        key: ValueKey(list.length),
                        style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize: 13),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              _pillRow(types.entries.toList(), type, (k) => setState(() => type = k)),
              if (genres.isNotEmpty) ...[
                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                _pillRow(
                  [const MapEntry('', 'Any genre'), ...(genres.toList()..sort()).map((g) => MapEntry(g, g))],
                  genre,
                  (k) => setState(() => genre = k),
                ),
              ],
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              Expanded(
                child: list.isEmpty
                    ? emptyScrollable(const EmptyState(
                        icon: Icons.movie_filter_outlined,
                        title: 'Nothing here yet',
                        subtitle: 'Try another filter, or pull down to refresh.',
                      ))
                    : Pull(child: posterGrid(list)),
              ),
            ],
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}

class SearchScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const SearchScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<SearchScreen> createState() => _SearchScreenState();
}

class _SearchScreenState extends State<SearchScreen> {
  final _ctl = TextEditingController();
  Timer? _debounce;
  String q = '';
  List<String> recents = [];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    SharedPreferences.getInstance().then((sp) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => recents = sp.getStringList('recent_searches') ?? []);
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _debounce?.cancel();
    _ctl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _onChanged(String v) {
    _debounce?.cancel();
    _debounce = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 220), () {
      if (mounted) setState(() => q = v.trim().toLowerCase());
    });
    setState(() {}); // refresh the clear button immediately
  }

  Future<void> _saveRecent(String v) async {
    final t = v.trim();
    if (t.isEmpty) return;
    final l = [t, ...recents.where((e) => e.toLowerCase() != t.toLowerCase())].take(8).toList();
    setState(() => recents = l);
    final sp = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await sp.setStringList('recent_searches', l);
  }

  Future<void> _clearRecents() async {
    setState(() => recents = []);
    final sp = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await sp.remove('recent_searches');
  }

  void _use(String s) {
    _ctl.text = s;
    _ctl.selection = TextSelection.collapsed(offset: s.length);
    setState(() => q = s.toLowerCase());
    FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
  }

  bool _match(Item e) {
    if ('${e['title'] ?? ''}'.toLowerCase().contains(q)) return true;
    if ('${e['year'] ?? ''}' == q) return true;
    final g = e['genres'];
    return g is List && g.any((x) => '$x'.toLowerCase().contains(q));
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SafeArea(
      bottom: false,
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 14, 16, 8),
            child: TextField(
              controller: _ctl,
              onChanged: _onChanged,
              textInputAction: TextInputAction.search,
              onSubmitted: (v) {
                _debounce?.cancel();
                setState(() => q = v.trim().toLowerCase());
                _saveRecent(v);
              },
              decoration: InputDecoration(
                hintText: 'Search movies, series, genres…',
                hintStyle: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white38),
                prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search_rounded),
                suffixIcon: _ctl.text.isEmpty
                    ? null
                    : IconButton(
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.close_rounded, size: 20),
                        onPressed: () {
                          _ctl.clear();
                          _debounce?.cancel();
                          setState(() => q = '');
                        },
                      ),
                filled: true,
                fillColor: kCard,
                contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
                border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16), borderSide: BorderSide.none),
                focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                  borderSide: const BorderSide(color: kRed, width: 1.2),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          Expanded(
            child: ValueListenableBuilder<List<Item>>(
              valueListenable: Store.content,
              builder: (_, all, __) {
                if (q.isEmpty) return _idle(all);
                final res = all.where(_match).toList();
                if (res.isEmpty) {
                  return emptyScrollable(EmptyState(
                    icon: Icons.search_off_rounded,
                    title: 'No results',
                    subtitle: 'Nothing matched “${_ctl.text.trim()}”.\nTry a different title or genre.',
                  ));
                }
                return Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 4, 16, 0),
                      child: Text('${res.length} result${res.length == 1 ? '' : 's'}',
                          style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize: 13)),
                    ),
                    Expanded(child: Pull(child: posterGrid(res))),
                  ],
                );
              },
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _idle(List<Item> all) {
    final top = all.where((e) => e['rating'] is num && (e['rating'] as num) > 0).toList()
      ..sort((a, b) => (b['rating'] as num).compareTo(a['rating'] as num));
    return Pull(
      child: CustomScrollView(
        physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
        keyboardDismissBehavior: ScrollViewKeyboardDismissBehavior.onDrag,
        slivers: [
          if (recents.isNotEmpty)
            SliverToBoxAdapter(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 8, 8, 4),
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        const Expanded(
                          child: Text('Recent', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
                        ),
                        TextButton(
                          onPressed: _clearRecents,
                          style: TextButton.styleFrom(foregroundColor: Colors.white54),
                          child: const Text('Clear'),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
                    child: Wrap(
                      spacing: 8,
                      runSpacing: 8,
                      children: [
                        for (final r in recents)
                          ActionChip(
                            avatar: const Icon(Icons.history_rounded, size: 16, color: Colors.white54),
                            label: Text(r),
                            backgroundColor: kCard,
                            side: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white10),
                            shape: const StadiumBorder(),
                            onPressed: () => _use(r),
                          ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          if (top.isNotEmpty) ...[
            SliverToBoxAdapter(child: sectionHeader('Top rated')),
            SliverPadding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, 24),
              sliver: posterSliver(top.take(18).toList()),
            ),
          ] else if (recents.isEmpty)
            const SliverFillRemaining(
              hasScrollBody: false,
              child: EmptyState(
                icon: Icons.search_rounded,
                title: 'Find something to watch',
                subtitle: 'Search by title, genre or year.',
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
