const kAvatarColors = <List<Color>>[
  [Color(0xFFFF4458), Color(0xFF9B0F1D)],
  [Color(0xFFFF9A3C), Color(0xFFE5501B)],
  [Color(0xFF29B6F6), Color(0xFF0D47A1)],
  [Color(0xFF66BB6A), Color(0xFF1B5E20)],
  [Color(0xFFAB47BC), Color(0xFF4A148C)],
  [Color(0xFFEC407A), Color(0xFF880E4F)],
  [Color(0xFF26C6DA), Color(0xFF006064)],
  [Color(0xFF8D6E63), Color(0xFF3E2723)],
];

class Avatar extends StatelessWidget {
  final String name;
  final int color;
  final double size;
  const Avatar({super.key, required this.name, this.color = 0, this.size = 96});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = kAvatarColors[color.clamp(0, kAvatarColors.length - 1)];
    return Container(
      width: size,
      height: size,
      alignment: Alignment.center,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        shape: BoxShape.circle,
        gradient: LinearGradient(begin: Alignment.topLeft, end: Alignment.bottomRight, colors: c),
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.white24, width: 3),
        boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: c.first.withOpacity(0.5), blurRadius: 24)],
      ),
      child: Text(
        name.isEmpty ? '?' : name.substring(0, 1).toUpperCase(),
        style: TextStyle(fontSize: size * 0.42, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: Colors.white),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class ProfileScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  const ProfileScreen({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SafeArea(
      bottom: false,
      child: Pull(
        child: ListView(
          physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 12, 16, 110),
          children: [
            ValueListenableBuilder<Item>(
              valueListenable: UserData.profile,
              builder: (_, p, __) => _header(context, p),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 18),
            _stats(),
            const SizedBox(height: 22),
            _lastWatch(context),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            _label('My library'),
            _tile(context, Icons.history_rounded, 'Watch history', const Color(0xFF29B6F6),
                () => _open(context, const LibraryScreen(kind: LibKind.history))),
            _tile(context, Icons.bookmark_rounded, 'Watchlist', const Color(0xFFFFB300),
                () => _open(context, const LibraryScreen(kind: LibKind.watchlist))),
            _tile(context, Icons.favorite_rounded, 'Favorites', kRedLight,
                () => _open(context, const LibraryScreen(kind: LibKind.favorites))),
            _tile(context, Icons.thumb_up_rounded, 'Liked', const Color(0xFF66BB6A),
                () => _open(context, const LibraryScreen(kind: LibKind.likes))),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            _label('Settings'),
            _tile(context, Icons.notifications_none_rounded, 'Notifications', Colors.white70,
                () => _open(context, const NotificationsScreen())),
            _tile(context, Icons.info_outline, 'About Cheetah', Colors.white70, () {
              showAboutDialog(
                context: context,
                applicationName: 'Cheetah',
                applicationVersion: '1.0.0',
                applicationIcon: ClipRRect(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  child: Image.asset('assets/icon.png', width: 48, height: 48),
                ),
              );
            }),
            _tile(context, Icons.logout, 'Log out', Colors.redAccent, () async {
              final ok = await showDialog<bool>(
                context: context,
                builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
                  title: const Text('Log out?'),
                  actions: [
                    TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false), child: const Text('Cancel')),
                    TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true), child: const Text('Log out')),
                  ],
                ),
              );
              if (ok == true) await FirebaseAuth.instance.signOut();
            }, color: Colors.redAccent),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _open(BuildContext c, Widget w) => Navigator.push(c, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => w));

  Widget _header(BuildContext context, Item p) {
    final u = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser;
    final name = (u?.displayName ?? '').isNotEmpty ? u!.displayName! : 'Cheetah user';
    final color = p['avatar'] is int ? p['avatar'] as int : 0;
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 22, 16, 18),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(28),
        gradient: LinearGradient(
          begin: Alignment.topLeft,
          end: Alignment.bottomRight,
          colors: [kRed.withOpacity(0.28), kCard],
        ),
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Avatar(name: name, color: color),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Text(name, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 22, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
          const SizedBox(height: 2),
          Text(u?.email ?? '', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54)),
          if ('${p['bio'] ?? ''}'.isNotEmpty) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text('${p['bio']}', textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70)),
          ],
          const SizedBox(height: 14),
          OutlinedButton.icon(
            onPressed: () => _open(context, const EditProfileScreen()),
            icon: const Icon(Icons.edit_rounded, size: 18),
            label: const Text('Edit profile'),
            style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
              foregroundColor: Colors.white,
              side: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white24),
              shape: const StadiumBorder(),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 22, vertical: 10),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _stats() {
    Widget cell(String label, ValueNotifier<Map<String, Item>> n) => Expanded(
          child: ValueListenableBuilder<Map<String, Item>>(
            valueListenable: n,
            builder: (_, m, __) => Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(color: kCard, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18)),
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  Text('${m.length}', style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 2),
                  Text(label, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: Colors.white54)),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        );
    return Row(children: [
      cell('Watched', UserData.history),
      const SizedBox(width: 10),
      cell('Watchlist', UserData.watchlist),
      const SizedBox(width: 10),
      cell('Favorites', UserData.favorites),
    ]);
  }

  Widget _lastWatch(BuildContext context) {
    return ValueListenableBuilder<Map<String, Item>>(
      valueListenable: UserData.history,
      builder: (_, m, __) {
        final l = UserData.sorted(m);
        if (l.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();
        return Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            _label('Last watched'),
            HistoryTile(item: l.first, big: true),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _label(String t) => Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(4, 4, 0, 10),
        child: Text(t.toUpperCase(),
            style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, letterSpacing: 1.2, color: Colors.white38, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
      );

  Widget _tile(BuildContext context, IconData i, String t, Color ic, VoidCallback on, {Color? color}) => Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
        child: Material(
          color: kCard,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          child: ListTile(
            leading: Container(
              width: 38,
              height: 38,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(color: ic.withOpacity(0.16), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
              child: Icon(i, color: ic, size: 22),
            ),
            title: Text(t, style: TextStyle(color: color, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
            trailing: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right, color: Colors.white38),
            onTap: on,
            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
          ),
        ),
      );
}

class EditProfileScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const EditProfileScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<EditProfileScreen> createState() => _EditProfileScreenState();
}

class _EditProfileScreenState extends State<EditProfileScreen> {
  late final TextEditingController name;
  late final TextEditingController bio;
  late int color;
  bool saving = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final u = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser;
    final p = UserData.profile.value;
    name = TextEditingController(text: u?.displayName ?? '');
    bio = TextEditingController(text: '${p['bio'] ?? ''}');
    color = p['avatar'] is int ? p['avatar'] as int : 0;
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    name.dispose();
    bio.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _save() async {
    final n = name.text.trim();
    if (n.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('Please enter a name')));
      return;
    }
    setState(() => saving = true);
    try {
      await FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser?.updateDisplayName(n);
      await UserData.saveProfile({'name': n, 'bio': bio.text.trim(), 'avatar': color});
      if (mounted) Navigator.pop(context);
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => saving = false);
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('Could not save. Try again.')));
      }
    }
  }

  InputDecoration _dec(String l, IconData i) => InputDecoration(
        labelText: l,
        prefixIcon: Icon(i),
        filled: true,
        fillColor: kCard,
        border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16), borderSide: BorderSide.none),
      );

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Edit profile', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800))),
      body: ListView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
        children: [
          Center(child: Avatar(name: name.text, color: color, size: 104)),
          const SizedBox(height: 18),
          SizedBox(
            height: 48,
            child: ListView.separated(
              scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
              itemCount: kAvatarColors.length,
              separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(width: 12),
              itemBuilder: (_, i) => GestureDetector(
                onTap: () => setState(() => color = i),
                child: Container(
                  width: 48,
                  height: 48,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    shape: BoxShape.circle,
                    gradient: LinearGradient(colors: kAvatarColors[i]),
                    border: Border.all(color: i == color ? Colors.white : Colors.transparent, width: 3),
                  ),
                  child: i == color ? const Icon(Icons.check_rounded) : null,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 26),
          TextField(
            controller: name,
            onChanged: (_) => setState(() {}),
            textCapitalization: TextCapitalization.words,
            decoration: _dec('Name', Icons.person_outline_rounded),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 14),
          TextField(
            controller: bio,
            maxLength: 80,
            decoration: _dec('Bio (optional)', Icons.short_text_rounded),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          TextField(
            controller: TextEditingController(text: FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser?.email ?? ''),
            enabled: false,
            decoration: _dec('Email', Icons.mail_outline_rounded),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          saving
              ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
              : GradientButton(icon: Icons.check_rounded, label: 'Save changes', onTap: _save),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class NotificationsScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  const NotificationsScreen({super.key});

  String _date(dynamic t) {
    if (t is! Timestamp) return '';
    final d = t.toDate();
    return '${d.day}/${d.month}/${d.year}';
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Notifications')),
      body: ValueListenableBuilder<List<Item>>(
        valueListenable: Store.notifs,
        builder: (_, list, __) {
          if (list.isEmpty) {
            return const Center(child: Text('No notifications', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white54)));
          }
          return Pull(child: ListView.separated(
            physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            itemCount: list.length,
            separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(height: 10),
            itemBuilder: (_, i) {
              final n = list[i];
              final it = Store.content.value.where((e) => e['id'] == n['contentId']).firstOrNull;
              return Material(
                color: kCard,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                child: InkWell(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                  onTap: it == null ? null : () => openItem(context, it),
                  child: Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        if ('${n['image'] ?? ''}'.isNotEmpty)
                          Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 12),
                            child: ClipRRect(
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                              child: SizedBox(width: 72, height: 72, child: Img(n['image']?.toString())),
                            ),
                          ),
                        Expanded(
                          child: Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Text('${n['title'] ?? ''}',
                                  style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 15)),
                              if ('${n['body'] ?? ''}'.isNotEmpty)
                                Padding(
                                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 3),
                                  child: Text('${n['body']}', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70)),
                                ),
                              Padding(
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 6),
                                child: Text(_date(n['createdAt']),
                                    style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white38, fontSize: 11)),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              );
            },
          ));
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}

########## FILE: lib/detail.dart ##########
import 'package:android_intent_plus/android_intent.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:webview_flutter/webview_flutter.dart';

import 'store.dart';
import 'theme.dart';
import 'userdata.dart';
import 'widgets.dart';

class DetailScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final Item item;
  const DetailScreen({super.key, required this.item});

  @override
  State<DetailScreen> createState() => _DetailScreenState();
}

class _DetailScreenState extends State<DetailScreen> {
  Item get it => widget.item;
  Map<String, dynamic>? omdb;
  int season = 1, episode = 1, seasonCount = 1, epCount = 1, serverIdx = 0;
  List<String> epNames = [];
  bool playing = false;

  bool get isTv => it['type'] == 'tv';
  String get tmdbId => '${it['tmdbId'] ?? ''}';

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    // Resume from the last watched episode.
    final h = UserData.history.value['${it['id']}'];
    if (h != null && isTv) {
      season = (h['season'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 1;
      episode = (h['episode'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 1;
    }
    _loadExtra();
  }

  Future<void> _loadExtra() async {
    if (tmdbId.isEmpty) return;
    String imdb = '';
    if (isTv) {
      final d = await Api.tmdb('tv/$tmdbId', {'append_to_response': 'external_ids'});
      if (d == null) return;
      imdb = '${d['external_ids']?['imdb_id'] ?? ''}';
      final n = (d['number_of_seasons'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 1;
      if (mounted) setState(() => seasonCount = n < 1 ? 1 : n);
      _loadEpisodes();
    } else {
      final d = await Api.tmdb('movie/$tmdbId');
      imdb = '${d?['imdb_id'] ?? ''}';
    }
    final o = await Api.omdb(imdb);
    if (mounted && o != null) setState(() => omdb = o);
  }

  Future<void> _loadEpisodes() async {
    final d = await Api.tmdb('tv/$tmdbId/season/$season');
    final eps = d?['episodes'];
    if (eps is List && mounted) {
      setState(() {
        epCount = eps.isEmpty ? 1 : eps.length;
        epNames = [for (final e in eps) '${e is Map ? (e['name'] ?? '') : ''}'];
        if (episode > epCount) episode = 1;
      });
    }
  }

  String _fill(String tpl) => tpl
      .replaceAll('{tmdb_id}', tmdbId)
      .replaceAll('{season}', '$season')
      .replaceAll('{episode}', '$episode');

  /// Global servers (from the admin panel) + any servers added to this title.
  List<Map<String, String>> get servers {
    final l = <Map<String, String>>[];
    if (tmdbId.isNotEmpty) {
      for (final s in Store.servers.value.where((s) => s['active'] != false)) {
        final tpl = '${(isTv ? s['tvUrl'] : s['movieUrl']) ?? ''}';
        if (tpl.isEmpty) continue;
        l.add({'name': '${s['name'] ?? 'Server'}', 'url': _fill(tpl)});
      }
    }
    final own = it['servers'];
    if (own is List) {
      for (final m in own) {
        if (m is Map && '${m['url'] ?? ''}'.isNotEmpty) {
          l.add({'name': '${m['name'] ?? 'Server'}', 'url': _fill('${m['url']}')});
        }
      }
    }
    return l;
  }

  Widget _pill(String t, {IconData? icon, Color? color}) => Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 5),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: kCard,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
          border: Border.all(color: Colors.white10),
        ),
        child: Row(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            if (icon != null) ...[Icon(icon, size: 14, color: color), const SizedBox(width: 4)],
            Text(t, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: Colors.white70)),
          ],
        ),
      );

  // ───────────── Action row: like / favorite / watchlist / cast ─────────────

  Widget _action(IconData on, IconData off, String label, bool active, Color activeColor, VoidCallback tap) {
    return Expanded(
      child: GestureDetector(
        behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
        onTap: () {
          HapticFeedback.lightImpact();
          tap();
        },
        child: Column(
          children: [
            AnimatedContainer(
              duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 220),
              width: 52,
              height: 52,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: active ? activeColor.withOpacity(0.18) : kCard,
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                border: Border.all(color: active ? activeColor : Colors.white10, width: 1.4),
              ),
              child: AnimatedSwitcher(
                duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 200),
                transitionBuilder: (c, a) => ScaleTransition(scale: a, child: c),
                child: Icon(active ? on : off,
                    key: ValueKey(active), color: active ? activeColor : Colors.white70, size: 24),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 6),
            Text(label,
                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: active ? activeColor : Colors.white60, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _actions() {
    return AnimatedBuilder(
      animation: Listenable.merge([UserData.likes, UserData.favorites, UserData.watchlist]),
      builder: (_, __) => Row(
        children: [
          _action(Icons.thumb_up_rounded, Icons.thumb_up_outlined, 'Like', UserData.isLiked(it),
              const Color(0xFF29B6F6), () => UserData.toggleLike(it)),
          _action(Icons.favorite_rounded, Icons.favorite_border_rounded, 'Favorite', UserData.isFav(it),
              kRedLight, () => UserData.toggleFav(it)),
          _action(Icons.bookmark_rounded, Icons.bookmark_border_rounded, 'Watchlist', UserData.inList(it),
              const Color(0xFFFFB300), () => UserData.toggleList(it)),
          _action(Icons.cast_rounded, Icons.cast_rounded, 'Cast', false, kRedLight, _cast),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  /// Opens the system Cast / Smart View screen so the phone screen can be sent
  /// to any Chromecast / Android TV / Miracast TV. Start the video first, then
  /// choose the TV.
  Future<void> _cast() async {
    try {
      await const AndroidIntent(action: 'android.settings.CAST_SETTINGS').launch();
    } catch (_) {
      try {
        await const AndroidIntent(action: 'android.settings.WIFI_DISPLAY_SETTINGS').launch();
      } catch (_) {
        if (mounted) {
          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
              const SnackBar(content: Text('Open Quick Settings and tap Cast / Smart View.')));
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // ───────────── Seasons & episodes ─────────────

  Widget _seasonRow() {
    return SizedBox(
      height: 40,
      child: ListView.separated(
        scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
        physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
        itemCount: seasonCount,
        separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(width: 8),
        itemBuilder: (_, i) {
          final s = i + 1;
          final sel = s == season;
          return GestureDetector(
            onTap: () {
              if (sel) return;
              setState(() {
                season = s;
                episode = 1;
                epNames = [];
              });
              _loadEpisodes();
              if (playing) UserData.addHistory(it, season: season, episode: episode);
            },
            child: AnimatedContainer(
              duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 220),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 18),
              alignment: Alignment.center,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                gradient: sel ? kRedGradient : null,
                color: sel ? null : kCard,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                border: Border.all(color: sel ? Colors.transparent : Colors.white10),
              ),
              child: Text('Season $s',
                  style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: sel ? Colors.white : Colors.white70)),
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  void _pickEpisode(int e) {
    setState(() => episode = e);
    if (playing) UserData.addHistory(it, season: season, episode: episode);
  }

  Widget _episodeRow() {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Row(
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: Text('Episodes · $epCount',
                  style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 15)),
            ),
            if (epCount > 6)
              TextButton.icon(
                onPressed: _allEpisodes,
                icon: const Icon(Icons.grid_view_rounded, size: 16),
                label: const Text('All'),
                style: TextButton.styleFrom(foregroundColor: kRedLight, visualDensity: VisualDensity.compact),
              ),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        SizedBox(
          height: 74,
          child: ListView.separated(
            key: PageStorageKey('eps$season'),
            scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
            physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
            itemCount: epCount,
            separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(width: 10),
            itemBuilder: (_, i) {
              final e = i + 1;
              final sel = e == episode;
              final name = i < epNames.length ? epNames[i] : '';
              return GestureDetector(
                onTap: () => _pickEpisode(e),
                child: AnimatedContainer(
                  duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 220),
                  width: 132,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: sel ? kRed.withOpacity(0.16) : kCard,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                    border: Border.all(color: sel ? kRed : Colors.white10, width: sel ? 1.6 : 1),
                  ),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                    children: [
                      Row(children: [
                        if (sel) const Padding(
                          padding: EdgeInsets.only(right: 4),
                          child: Icon(Icons.play_arrow_rounded, size: 18, color: kRedLight),
                        ),
                        Text('EP $e',
                            style: TextStyle(
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: sel ? kRedLight : Colors.white, fontSize: 14)),
                      ]),
                      if (name.isNotEmpty) ...[
                        const SizedBox(height: 3),
                        Text(name,
                            maxLines: 1,
                            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                            style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11.5, color: Colors.white54)),
                      ],
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              );
            },
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  void _allEpisodes() {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      backgroundColor: kCard,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24))),
      builder: (ctx) => DraggableScrollableSheet(
        expand: false,
        initialChildSize: 0.6,
        maxChildSize: 0.9,
        builder: (_, sc) => Column(
          children: [
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            Container(width: 40, height: 4, decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.white24, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2))),
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              child: Align(
                alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                child: Text('Season $season · $epCount episodes',
                    style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 17, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
              ),
            ),
            Expanded(
              child: GridView.builder(
                controller: sc,
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, 24),
                gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(
                    crossAxisCount: 5, mainAxisSpacing: 10, crossAxisSpacing: 10),
                itemCount: epCount,
                itemBuilder: (_, i) {
                  final e = i + 1;
                  final sel = e == episode;
                  return GestureDetector(
                    onTap: () {
                      Navigator.pop(ctx);
                      _pickEpisode(e);
                    },
                    child: Container(
                      alignment: Alignment.center,
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        gradient: sel ? kRedGradient : null,
                        color: sel ? null : kCard2,
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                      ),
                      child: Text('$e', style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 16)),
                    ),
                  );
                },
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  /// Titles that share genres with this one.
  List<Item> get related {
    final g = it['genres'];
    final mine = g is List ? g.map((e) => '$e').toSet() : <String>{};
    if (mine.isEmpty) return [];
    int score(Item e) {
      final x = e['genres'];
      return x is List ? x.where((a) => mine.contains('$a')).length : 0;
    }

    final l = Store.content.value.where((e) => e['id'] != it['id'] && score(e) > 0).toList();
    l.sort((a, b) => score(b).compareTo(score(a)));
    return l.take(12).toList();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final sv = servers;
    final idx = sv.isEmpty ? 0 : (serverIdx >= sv.length ? 0 : serverIdx);
    final url = sv.isEmpty ? '' : sv[idx]['url']!;
    final g = it['genres'];
    final genres = g is List ? g.map((e) => '$e').toList() : <String>[];
    final backdrop = '${it['backdrop'] ?? ''}'.isNotEmpty ? '${it['backdrop']}' : '${it['poster'] ?? ''}';
    final rating = it['rating'] is num ? (it['rating'] as num).toDouble() : 0.0;
    final imdb = '${omdb?['imdbRating'] ?? ''}';

    return Scaffold(
      body: SafeArea(
        bottom: false,
        child: ListView(
          physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 32),
          children: [
            AspectRatio(
              aspectRatio: 16 / 9,
              child: Stack(
                fit: StackFit.expand,
                children: [
                  if (playing && url.isNotEmpty)
                    PlayerView(key: ValueKey(url), url: url)
                  else ...[
                    Img(backdrop),
                    const DecoratedBox(
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        gradient: LinearGradient(
                          begin: Alignment.topCenter,
                          end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
                          colors: [Color(0x66000000), Colors.transparent, kBg],
                          stops: [0, 0.4, 1],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    Center(
                      child: GestureDetector(
                        onTap: _play,
                        child: Container(
                          width: 68,
                          height: 68,
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            gradient: kRedGradient,
                            shape: BoxShape.circle,
                            border: Border.all(color: Colors.white24, width: 2),
                            boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: kRed.withOpacity(0.55), blurRadius: 28)],
                          ),
                          child: const Icon(Icons.play_arrow_rounded, size: 42),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                  Positioned(
                    top: 8,
                    left: 8,
                    child: Material(
                      color: Colors.black54,
                      shape: const CircleBorder(),
                      child: IconButton(
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back_rounded),
                        onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  Positioned(
                    top: 8,
                    right: 8,
                    child: Material(
                      color: Colors.black54,
                      shape: const CircleBorder(),
                      child: IconButton(
                        tooltip: 'Cast to TV',
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.cast_rounded),
                        onPressed: _cast,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 14, 16, 0),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text('${it['title'] ?? ''}',
                      style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 24, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, height: 1.15)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  Wrap(
                    spacing: 8,
                    runSpacing: 8,
                    children: [
                      if ('${it['year'] ?? ''}'.isNotEmpty) _pill('${it['year']}'),
                      if (rating > 0)
                        _pill('TMDB ${rating.toStringAsFixed(1)}', icon: Icons.star_rounded, color: Colors.amber),
                      if (imdb.isNotEmpty && imdb != 'N/A')
                        _pill('IMDb $imdb', icon: Icons.star_rounded, color: Colors.amber),
                      if ('${omdb?['Runtime'] ?? ''}'.isNotEmpty && omdb?['Runtime'] != 'N/A')
                        _pill('${omdb?['Runtime']}', icon: Icons.schedule_rounded, color: Colors.white54),
                      if ('${omdb?['Rated'] ?? ''}'.isNotEmpty && omdb?['Rated'] != 'N/A')
                        _pill('${omdb?['Rated']}'),
                      for (final x in genres) _pill(x),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 18),
                  _actions(),
                  if (isTv && tmdbId.isNotEmpty) ...[
                    const SizedBox(height: 20),
                    _seasonRow(),
                    const SizedBox(height: 14),
                    _episodeRow(),
                    if (playing && episode < epCount) ...[
                      const SizedBox(height: 10),
                      OutlinedButton.icon(
                        onPressed: () => _pickEpisode(episode + 1),
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.skip_next_rounded),
                        label: const Text('Next episode'),
                        style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                          foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                          side: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white24),
                          shape: const StadiumBorder(),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ],
                  const SizedBox(height: 18),
                  if (sv.isEmpty)
                    const Text('No server available for this title yet.',
                        style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white54))
                  else ...[
                    const Text('Servers', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 15)),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    Wrap(
                      spacing: 8,
                      runSpacing: 4,
                      children: [
                        for (var i = 0; i < sv.length; i++)
                          ChoiceChip(
                            avatar: Icon(Icons.dns_rounded, size: 16, color: i == idx ? Colors.white : Colors.white54),
                            label: Text(sv[i]['name']!),
                            selected: i == idx,
                            showCheckmark: false,
                            selectedColor: kRed,
                            backgroundColor: kCard,
                            side: BorderSide.none,
                            shape: const StadiumBorder(),
                            onSelected: (_) => setState(() => serverIdx = i),
                          ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ],
                  if (!playing && sv.isNotEmpty) ...[
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    SizedBox(
                      width: double.infinity,
                      child: GradientButton(
                        icon: Icons.play_arrow_rounded,
                        label: isTv ? 'Play S$season · E$episode' : 'Play',
                        onTap: _play,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                  if ('${it['overview'] ?? ''}'.isNotEmpty) ...[
                    const SizedBox(height: 20),
                    const Text('Overview', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, fontSize: 15)),
                    const SizedBox(height: 6),
                    _Overview('${it['overview']}'),
                  ],
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const AdBanner('detail'),
            hRow(context, 'More like this', related),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _play() {
    if (servers.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
          .showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('No server available for this title yet.')));
      return;
    }
    setState(() => playing = true);
    UserData.addHistory(it, season: season, episode: episode);
  }
}

class _Overview extends StatefulWidget {
  final String text;
  const _Overview(this.text);

  @override
  State<_Overview> createState() => _OverviewState();
}

class _OverviewState extends State<_Overview> {
  bool open = false;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    const style = TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, height: 1.5);
    return LayoutBuilder(
      builder: (_, box) {
        final tp = TextPainter(
          text: TextSpan(text: widget.text, style: style),
          maxLines: 4,
          textDirection: TextDirection.ltr,
        )..layout(maxWidth: box.maxWidth);
        final long = tp.didExceedMaxLines;
        return Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            AnimatedSize(
              duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 220),
              curve: Curves.easeOut,
              alignment: Alignment.topCenter,
              child: Text(
                widget.text,
                style: style,
                maxLines: open ? null : 4,
                overflow: open ? TextOverflow.visible : TextOverflow.ellipsis,
              ),
            ),
            if (long)
              GestureDetector(
                onTap: () => setState(() => open = !open),
                child: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 6),
                  child: Text(open ? 'Show less' : 'Read more',
                      style: const TextStyle(color: kRedLight, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
                ),
              ),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

/// Embedded player. Blocks pop-up style redirects to other sites once the
/// player page has finished loading.
class PlayerView extends StatefulWidget {
  final String url;
  const PlayerView({super.key, required this.url});

  @override
  State<PlayerView> createState() => _PlayerViewState();
}

class _PlayerViewState extends State<PlayerView> {
  late final WebViewController c;
  bool loading = true;
  bool loaded = false;

  String _root(String h) {
    final p = h.split('.');
    return p.length >= 2 ? p.sublist(p.length - 2).join('.') : h;
  }

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final base = _root(Uri.tryParse(widget.url)?.host ?? '');
    c = WebViewController()
      ..setJavaScriptMode(JavaScriptMode.unrestricted)
      ..setBackgroundColor(Colors.black)
      ..setNavigationDelegate(NavigationDelegate(
        onPageFinished: (_) {
          loaded = true;
          if (mounted) setState(() => loading = false);
        },
        onNavigationRequest: (r) {
          if (!loaded || !r.isMainFrame) return NavigationDecision.navigate;
          final u = Uri.tryParse(r.url);
          if (u == null) return NavigationDecision.prevent;
          if (u.scheme == 'about' || u.scheme == 'data' || _root(u.host) == base) {
            return NavigationDecision.navigate;
          }
          return NavigationDecision.prevent;
        },
      ));
    final lower = widget.url.toLowerCase().split('?').first;
    if (lower.endsWith('.mp4') || lower.endsWith('.webm')) {
      c.loadHtmlString(
          '<html><body style="margin:0;background:#000"><video src="${widget.url}" controls autoplay playsinline style="width:100%;height:100%"></video></body></html>');
    } else {
      c.loadRequest(Uri.parse(widget.url));
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Stack(
      fit: StackFit.expand,
      children: [
        WebViewWidget(controller: c),
        if (loading) const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
      ],
    );
  }
}

########## FILE: lib/userdata.dart ##########
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

import 'store.dart';

/// Per-user data: likes, favorites, watchlist, watch history.
/// Stored under users/{uid}/{likes|favorites|watchlist|history}/{contentId}.
class UserData {
  static final likes = ValueNotifier<Map<String, Item>>({});
  static final favorites = ValueNotifier<Map<String, Item>>({});
  static final watchlist = ValueNotifier<Map<String, Item>>({});
  static final history = ValueNotifier<Map<String, Item>>({});
  static final profile = ValueNotifier<Item>({});

  static final List<StreamSubscription> _subs = [];
  static String? _uid;

  static DocumentReference<Map<String, dynamic>>? get _me =>
      _uid == null ? null : db.collection('users').doc(_uid);

  static void start() {
    final u = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser;
    if (u == null || _uid == u.uid) return;
    stop();
    _uid = u.uid;
    final me = _me!;
    _listen('likes', likes);
    _listen('favorites', favorites);
    _listen('watchlist', watchlist);
    _listen('history', history);
    _subs.add(me.snapshots().listen((d) => profile.value = d.data() ?? {}, onError: (_) {}));
  }

  static void _listen(String name, ValueNotifier<Map<String, Item>> n) {
    _subs.add(_me!.collection(name).snapshots().listen((s) {
      n.value = {for (final d in s.docs) d.id: <String, dynamic>{...d.data(), 'id': d.id}};
    }, onError: (_) {}));
  }

  static void stop() {
    for (final s in _subs) {
      s.cancel();
    }
    _subs.clear();
    _uid = null;
    likes.value = {};
    favorites.value = {};
    watchlist.value = {};
    history.value = {};
    profile.value = {};
  }

  /// Small snapshot of a title, enough to draw a card without the content list.
  static Map<String, dynamic> _snap(Item it) => {
        'title': it['title'],
        'poster': it['poster'],
        'backdrop': it['backdrop'],
        'type': it['type'],
        'year': it['year'],
        'rating': it['rating'],
      };

  static Future<void> _toggle(String col, Item it, bool on) async {
    final me = _me;
    if (me == null) return;
    final ref = me.collection(col).doc('${it['id']}');
    if (on) {
      await ref.delete();
    } else {
      await ref.set({..._snap(it), 'at': FieldValue.serverTimestamp()});
    }
  }

  static bool isLiked(Item it) => likes.value.containsKey('${it['id']}');
  static bool isFav(Item it) => favorites.value.containsKey('${it['id']}');
  static bool inList(Item it) => watchlist.value.containsKey('${it['id']}');

  static Future<void> toggleLike(Item it) => _toggle('likes', it, isLiked(it));
  static Future<void> toggleFav(Item it) => _toggle('favorites', it, isFav(it));
  static Future<void> toggleList(Item it) => _toggle('watchlist', it, inList(it));

  /// Called when playback starts / the episode changes.
  static Future<void> addHistory(Item it, {int season = 1, int episode = 1}) async {
    final me = _me;
    if (me == null) return;
    await me.collection('history').doc('${it['id']}').set({
      ..._snap(it),
      'season': season,
      'episode': episode,
      'at': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    });
  }

  static Future<void> removeHistory(String id) async => _me?.collection('history').doc(id).delete();

  static Future<void> clearHistory() async {
    final me = _me;
    if (me == null) return;
    final s = await me.collection('history').get();
    final b = db.batch();
    for (final d in s.docs) {
      b.delete(d.reference);
    }
    await b.commit();
  }

  static Future<void> saveProfile(Map<String, dynamic> m) async =>
      _me?.set(m, SetOptions(merge: true));

  static int _ms(Item m) {
    final t = m['at'];
    return t is Timestamp ? t.millisecondsSinceEpoch : DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch;
  }

  /// Newest first.
  static List<Item> sorted(Map<String, Item> m) {
    final l = m.values.toList();
    l.sort((a, b) => _ms(b).compareTo(_ms(a)));
    return l;
  }

  /// Full content item if it still exists, else the saved snapshot.
  static Item resolve(Item saved) {
    final full = Store.content.value.where((e) => e['id'] == saved['id']).firstOrNull;
    return full ?? saved;
  }
}

########## FILE: lib/library.dart ##########
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import 'store.dart';
import 'theme.dart';
import 'userdata.dart';
import 'widgets.dart';

enum LibKind { history, watchlist, favorites, likes }

extension on LibKind {
  String get title => switch (this) {
        LibKind.history => 'Watch history',
        LibKind.watchlist => 'Watchlist',
        LibKind.favorites => 'Favorites',
        LibKind.likes => 'Liked',
      };
  IconData get icon => switch (this) {
        LibKind.history => Icons.history_rounded,
        LibKind.watchlist => Icons.bookmark_rounded,
        LibKind.favorites => Icons.favorite_rounded,
        LibKind.likes => Icons.thumb_up_rounded,
      };
  ValueNotifier<Map<String, Item>> get source => switch (this) {
        LibKind.history => UserData.history,
        LibKind.watchlist => UserData.watchlist,
        LibKind.favorites => UserData.favorites,
        LibKind.likes => UserData.likes,
      };
  String get empty => switch (this) {
        LibKind.history => 'Nothing watched yet',
        LibKind.watchlist => 'Your watchlist is empty',
        LibKind.favorites => 'No favorites yet',
        LibKind.likes => 'No liked titles yet',
      };
}

class LibraryScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  final LibKind kind;
  const LibraryScreen({super.key, required this.kind});

  Future<void> _clear(BuildContext context) async {
    final ok = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        backgroundColor: kCard,
        title: const Text('Clear history?'),
        content: const Text('This removes everything you have watched.'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false), child: const Text('Cancel')),
          TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true), child: const Text('Clear')),
        ],
      ),
    );
    if (ok == true) await UserData.clearHistory();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(kind.title, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
        actions: [
          if (kind == LibKind.history)
            IconButton(
              tooltip: 'Clear all',
              icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_sweep_rounded),
              onPressed: () => _clear(context),
            ),
        ],
      ),
      body: ValueListenableBuilder<Map<String, Item>>(
        valueListenable: kind.source,
        builder: (_, map, __) {
          final list = UserData.sorted(map);
          if (list.isEmpty) {
            return Center(
              child: Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  Icon(kind.icon, size: 56, color: Colors.white24),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  Text(kind.empty, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize: 15)),
                ],
              ),
            );
          }
          if (kind == LibKind.history) return _historyList(context, list);
          return posterGrid([for (final e in list) UserData.resolve(e)]);
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _historyList(BuildContext context, List<Item> list) {
    return ListView.separated(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      itemCount: list.length,
      separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(height: 10),
      itemBuilder: (_, i) => HistoryTile(item: list[i], onRemove: () => UserData.removeHistory('${list[i]['id']}')),
    );
  }
}

/// Wide card used for history and "Continue watching".
class HistoryTile extends StatelessWidget {
  final Item item;
  final VoidCallback? onRemove;
  final bool big;
  const HistoryTile({super.key, required this.item, this.onRemove, this.big = false});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isTv = item['type'] == 'tv';
    final img = '${item['backdrop'] ?? ''}'.isNotEmpty ? '${item['backdrop']}' : '${item['poster'] ?? ''}';
    final sub = isTv ? 'Season ${item['season'] ?? 1} · Episode ${item['episode'] ?? 1}' : 'Movie';
    return Material(
      color: kCard,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
      clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: () => openItem(context, UserData.resolve(item)),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            SizedBox(
              width: big ? 150 : 120,
              height: big ? 90 : 72,
              child: Stack(
                fit: StackFit.expand,
                children: [
                  Img(img),
                  const Center(child: Icon(Icons.play_circle_fill_rounded, color: Colors.white70, size: 30)),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            Expanded(
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    Text('${item['title'] ?? ''}',
                        maxLines: 1,
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                        style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 15)),
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    Text(sub, style: const TextStyle(color: kRedLight, fontSize: 12.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
            if (onRemove != null)
              IconButton(
                icon: const Icon(Icons.close_rounded, color: Colors.white38),
                onPressed: onRemove,
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
